//! Business-state regressions for declarative day-two adoption.
use super::*;
use crate::{Context,Engine,WriteState,wire};
use crate::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;
use serde_json::{json,Value as Json};
fn compile(source:&str)->(Schema,Vec<crate::ir::Program>){
    let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    (serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&b.schema).unwrap()).unwrap(),b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap()).unwrap()).collect())
}
fn engine(source:&str)->Engine{let(s,p)=compile(source);Engine::new_release(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,1,None).unwrap()}
fn adopt(e:&mut Engine,source:&str,n:u64)->Result<u64,RuntimeError>{
    let(s,p)=compile(source);let previous=e.store().meta("release").unwrap().map(|s|s.parse().unwrap());
    e.adopt_release(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(n,previous),false)
}
fn seed(e:&mut Engine,table:&str,row:Json){let row=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[table],&row).unwrap();e.seed([(table.into(),row)]).unwrap();}
fn row(e:&mut Engine,table:&str,id:&str)->Value{e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap().get(table,id).unwrap().unwrap()}
fn field(e:&mut Engine,table:&str,id:&str,c:&str)->Value{row(e,table,id).field(c).unwrap().clone()}
const BASE:&str="table bookings:\n  weight: int\n  length: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  delete <- allow\n  online only\nmutation edit():\n  update bookings[id = 'a'] {weight:4000}\nmutation remove():\n  delete bookings[id = 'a']\n";
fn ferry()->String{BASE.replace("  weight: int","  vehicleClass: enum('car', 'van', 'commercial')\n  weight: int")+"backfill bookings vehicleClass(weight: int, length: int):\n  {vehicleClass: 'commercial' if weight > 3500 else 'van' if length >= 5 else 'car'}\nmutation classify():\n  update bookings[id = 'a'] {vehicleClass:'van'}\n"}
fn write(e:&mut Engine,op:&str){let r=e.mutate(op,None,Context::new("alice",Default::default())).unwrap();assert!(matches!(r.state,WriteState::Sent{..}),"{r:?}");}
#[test]
fn conditional_backfill_rollback_older_writes_and_forward_catchup(){
 let mut e=engine(BASE);seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","weight":1000,"length":4}));
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"b","weight":1000,"length":5}));
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"c","weight":4000,"length":4}));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();
 for(id,class)in[("a","car"),("b","van"),("c","commercial")]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings",id,"vehicleClass"),Value::String(class.into()));}
 assert!(e.store().adoption_report.backfills.iter().any(|s|s.contains("transformed 3 rows")));
 let g=e.generation();adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
 write(&mut e,"classify");
 adopt(&mut e,BASE,1).unwrap();assert!(row(&mut e,"bookings","a").field("vehicleClass").is_err());
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"d","weight":3600,"length":1}));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","vehicleClass"),Value::String("van".into()));
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","d","vehicleClass"),Value::String("commercial".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,BASE,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"edit");
 let before=e.generation();let error=adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("edited")&&error.contains("historical inputs changed"),"{error}");assert_eq!(before,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn older_input_change_recomputes_unedited_derived_value(){
 let mut e=engine(BASE);seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","weight":1000,"length":4}));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,BASE,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"edit");
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","vehicleClass"),Value::String("commercial".into()));
}
const CUE:&str="table cues:\n  status: enum('standby','done')\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n";
fn cues()->String{CUE.replace("enum('standby','done')","enum('standby','complete','skipped')")+"backfill cues rename(status: enum('standby','done')):\n  {status: 'complete' if status = 'done' else status}\nmutation skip():\n  update cues['a'] {status:'skipped'}\n"}
#[test]
fn enum_rename_reverses_exact_preimage_and_refuses_post_backfill_edits(){
 let mut e=engine(CUE);seed(&mut e,"cues",json!({"id":"a","status":"done"}));adopt(&mut e,&cues(),2).unwrap();
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("complete".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,CUE,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("done".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,&cues(),2).unwrap();write(&mut e,"skip");let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,CUE,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("changed after backfill"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("skipped".into()));
}
#[test]
fn backfill_and_evidence_roll_back_with_later_unique_failure(){
 let mut e=engine(BASE);for id in["a","b"]{seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":id,"weight":1000,"length":4}));}
 let bad=ferry().replace("  vehicleClass:","  unique vehicleClass\n  vehicleClass:");let g=e.generation();
 assert!(adopt(&mut e,&bad,2).is_err());assert_eq!(g,e.generation());assert!(row(&mut e,"bookings","a").field("vehicleClass").is_err());
 assert_ne!(e.store().meta("version").unwrap().as_deref(),Some(super::backfills::VERSION));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","vehicleClass"),Value::String("car".into()));
}
#[test]
fn deletion_and_id_reuse_never_inherit_an_old_receipt(){
 let mut e=engine(BASE);seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","weight":1000,"length":4}));adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,BASE,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"remove");seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","weight":5000,"length":4}));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","vehicleClass"),Value::String("commercial".into()));
}
#[test]
fn string_split_and_ascii_digit_classification_are_deterministic(){
 let source="table people:\n  name: text <=100\n  location: text <=100\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=source.replace("  name:","  first: text <=100\n  last: text <=100\n  room: bool\n  name:")+"backfill people parts(name: text <=100, location: text <=100):\n  {first: beforeLast(name, ' '), last: afterLast(name, ' '), room: allDigits(location)}\n";
 let mut e=engine(source);for(id,name,location)in[("a","Ada Lovelace","123"),("b","Prince","１２３"),("c","A B C","")]{seed(&mut e,"people",json!({"id":id,"name":name,"location":location}));}
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 for(id,first,last,room)in[("a","Ada","Lovelace",true),("b","Prince","",false),("c","A B","C",false)]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"first"),Value::String(first.into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"last"),Value::String(last.into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"room"),Value::Bool(room));}
}
#[test]
fn backfill_fragment_refuses_clock_viewer_scans_and_overlapping_outputs(){
 for value in["viewer","now","new"]{let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),format!("{BASE}backfill bookings nope():\n  {{weight:{value}}}\n"))]);assert!(!b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{value}");}
 let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),format!("{BASE}backfill bookings one():\n  {{weight:1}}\nbackfill bookings two():\n  {{weight:2}}\n"))]);assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.message.contains("overlap")));
}
#[test]
fn all_fifty_wave17_change_shapes_run_without_migration_programs(){
 let cases:Vec<Json>=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../tests/backfills/wave17.json")).unwrap();assert_eq!(cases.len(),50);
 for case in cases {
  let lane=case["lane"].as_u64().unwrap();let inputs=case["inputs"].as_object().unwrap();let outputs=case["outputs"].as_object().unwrap();
  let columns=|fields:&serde_json::Map<String,Json>|fields.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("  {c}: {}\n",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<String>();
  let old=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n",columns(inputs));
  let args=inputs.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("{c}: {}",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  let next=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill rows change{lane}({args}):\n  {}\n",columns(outputs),case["expression"].as_str().unwrap());
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),next.clone())]);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"lane {lane} {}: {:?}\n{next}",case["slug"],bound.diagnostics);
  let mut e=engine(&old);
  for (i,r) in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){let mut input=r["input"].as_object().unwrap().clone();input.insert("id".into(),json!(format!("r{i}")));seed(&mut e,"rows",Json::Object(input));}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane}: {error}\n{next}"));
  assert!(e.schema().public_projection().backfills.is_empty(),"historical transforms never enter device input");
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){
   let got=row(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"));
   for(c,wanted)in r["expect"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(got.field(c).unwrap()),*wanted,"lane {lane} row {i} column {c}");}
  }
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} rollback: {error}"));
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){for(c,wanted)in r["input"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(&field(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"),c)),*wanted,"lane {lane} restored {c}");}}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} forward: {error}"));
 }
}
#[test]
fn unique_lookup_uses_historical_rows_and_rechecks_changed_lookup_values(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  unique principal\n  read <- deny\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill bookings plot(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"A17"}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("A17".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 // Trusted seed replaces the source row as an operator update for this test.
 seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"B20"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("B20".into()));
}
#[test]
fn cross_reference_existence_is_evaluated_before_any_table_output_changes(){
 let old="table sightings:\n  tower: text <=20\n  reportedAt: time\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  tower:","  confidence: enum('confirmed','possible')\n  tower:")+"backfill sightings confidence(tower: text <=20, reportedAt: time):\n  {confidence:'confirmed' if exists sightings where .tower != tower and .reportedAt >= reportedAt - 15m and .reportedAt <= reportedAt + 15m else 'possible'}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"a","tower":"north","reportedAt":1000000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"b","tower":"south","reportedAt":1100000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"c","tower":"west","reportedAt":9000000}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","a","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","b","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","c","confidence"),Value::String("possible".into()));
}
#[test]
fn retained_table_reentry_never_feeds_a_transform_its_own_output(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for direct_old in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();
  if direct_old {adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
  else {adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
 }
}
#[test]
fn previously_narrowed_rows_join_the_backfill_source_snapshot(){
 let old="table facts:\n  principal: principal\n  n: int 0..20\n  unique principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("int 0..20","int 0..5");
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int").replace("  owner: principal","  score: int\n  owner: principal")+"backfill facts twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\nbackfill bookings scores(owner: principal):\n  {score:(facts[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"facts",json!({"id":"a","principal":"user:a","n":9}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"b","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("facts").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"facts","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","b","score"),Value::Int(9));
}
#[test]
fn historical_lookup_duplicates_refuse_before_another_patch_can_resolve_them(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  replacement: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  principal: principal","  unique principal\n  principal: principal").replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill members uniquePrincipals(replacement: principal):\n  {principal:replacement}\nbackfill bookings plots(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);for(id,replacement,plot)in[("a","user:a","A"),("b","user:b","B")]{seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":id,"principal":"user:x","replacement":replacement,"plot":plot}));}
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"c","owner":"user:x"}));let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical lookup")&&error.contains("not unique")&&error.contains("2 affected rows"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn pruning_preimages_is_visible_and_exact_reversal_then_refuses(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 let preview=e.store_mut().prune_dormant(false,None).unwrap();assert!(preview["items"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|i|i["kind"]=="backfill"&&i["rows"]==1));
 e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();let error=adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("explicitly pruned"),"{error}");assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));
}
#[test]
fn carried_backfills_apply_to_rows_restored_for_the_first_time(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  label: enum('a','b')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("enum('a','b')","enum('a')");
 let with_fill=narrow.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let restored=with_fill.replace("enum('a')","enum('a','b')");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":9,"label":"b"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&with_fill,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&restored,4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));
}

 assert!(!b.diagnostics.is_empty());
}
#[test]
fn pruned_active_membership_refuses_reentry_without_double_applying() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();
 let g=e.generation();let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("explicitly pruned"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn prune_drops_deleted_lifetime_receipts_before_id_reuse() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":10}));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(10));
}
#[test]
fn dropped_historical_reference_cannot_recapture_a_reused_parent() {
 let wide="table parents:\n  public 'fixture'\ntable children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  status: enum('live','new')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let mut e=engine(wide);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p"}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p","status":"new"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&wide.replace("enum('live','new')","enum('live')"),2).unwrap();
 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
 seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p"}));
 let next=wide.replace("  parent: parents on delete keep","  replacement: parents on delete keep")+"backfill children copy(parent: parents):\n  {replacement:parent}\n";
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}
#[test]
fn expired_unswept_source_cannot_extend_its_dropped_expiry() {
 let old="table items:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 for dormant in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);let r=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","until":50})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),r)],0).unwrap();
  if dormant {let(s,p)=compile("query empty():\n  return true\n");e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();}
  let next=old.replace("  expire at .until\n","")+"backfill items prolong(until: time):\n  {until:until + 1d}\n";
  adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 }
}

#[test]
fn absent_optional_output_is_not_a_native_image_with_missing_historical_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  derived: int ?\n  n: int")+"backfill items absent(missing: int):\n  {derived:missing}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.missing"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn removal_of_last_opening_boundary_keeps_the_durable_retirement_floor() {
 let old="table items:\n  opens: time\n  until: time\n  n: int\n  not before .opens\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  not before .opens\n","").replace("  expire at .until\n","")+"backfill items prolong(until: time):\n  {until:until + 1d}\n";
 for requested in [1,100] {
  let(s,p)=compile(old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let r=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","opens":0,"until":50,"n":3})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),r)],0).unwrap();
  {let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();tx.pin_time_audience_clock(100).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,requested).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0,"source deadline before persisted floor, requested={requested}");
 }
}

#[test]
fn a_new_named_declaration_transforms_existing_historical_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let triple=twice.replace("twice(n:","triple(n:").replace("n * 2","n * 3");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&triple,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(9));
}
#[test]
fn dropped_required_and_optional_inputs_survive_active_table_reentry() {
 for kind in ["int","int ?"] {
  let old=format!("table items:\n  n: {kind}\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let next=old.replace(&format!("  n: {kind}"),"  doubled: int")+&format!("backfill items twice(n: {kind}):\n  {{doubled:(n ?? 0) * 2}}\n");
  let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));
 }
}
#[test]
fn reentry_restores_shared_snapshots_for_arguments_and_point_reads() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for later in [false,true] {
  let copied=twice.replace("  n: int","  copied: int\n  lookedUp: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items copy(n: int, id: items):\n  {copied:n, lookedUp:(items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));
  if later {adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();}
  adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();let expected=if later {6}else{3};
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","lookedUp"),Value::Int(expected));
 }
}

#[test]
fn a_retained_transform_output_cannot_prove_native_origin() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let broken=twice.replace("twice(n: int)","other(missing: int)").replace("doubled:n * 2","doubled:missing");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&broken,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.missing"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn partial_table_reentry_restores_carried_declarations_in_the_same_cohort() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let parent_fill="backfill parents twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  parent: parents","  copied: int\n  parent: parents")+parent_fill+"backfill children copy(parent: parents):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,&(parents.to_owned()+parent_fill),3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"parents","p","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","copied"),Value::Int(3));
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Database-authoritative release history and explicit dormant pruning.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#4-explicit-pruning-and-audit — no implicit collector or unaudited data loss.
use super::*;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DormantItem {
    /// Stable cursor; column/index names may coincide.
    pub key: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub rows: u64,
    pub bytes: u64,
    pub asset_bytes: u64,
    /// Internal audit distinction: zero standalone cells may still be covered by a dormant row.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) definition_only: bool,
    pub sample_ids: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub after_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub restored_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_sample_ids:Option<Vec<String>>,
}

impl DormantItem {
    pub fn unit(&self) -> &'static str {
        match (self.kind.as_str(), self.rows) { ("backfill", 1) => "preimage", ("backfill", _) => "preimages", ("column", 1) => "cell", ("column", _) => "cells", ("index", 1) => "entry", ("index", _) => "entries", (_, 1) => "row", _ => "rows" }
    }
}

impl Store {
    /// Values presently in active rows, independently of table population or other columns.
    pub fn rollback_incompatible_values(&self, current: &Schema, target: &Schema, name: &str, column: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.authority_transaction()?;
        let table = &target.tables[name];
        let kind = &table.columns[column];
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) { return Ok((0, Vec::new())); }
        let mut count = 0;
        let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1) ORDER BY id")?;
        for data in statement.query_map([name], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))? {
            let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data?).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, &current.tables[name]);
            if let Ok(value) = row.field(column)
                && super::narrowed::incompatible_column(&tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? {
                count += 1;
                if samples.len() < 5 {
                    let text = match value { Value::String(s) => format!("'{}'", s.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\r', "\\r")), _ => canonical_json(value) };
                    samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&text));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
    }
}

fn sql_number(n: u64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    i64::try_from(n).map_err(|_| {
        RuntimeError::Generation("release counter exceeds SQLite integer range".into())
    })
}

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' AND name='_dormant_tables')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}
pub(crate) fn inventory(
    conn: &Connection,
    after: Option<&str>,
    limit: usize,
) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
    if !present(conn)? {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    }
    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        " UNION ALL SELECT c.name,'rows',c.name,NULL FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t GROUP BY n.t "
    } else {""};
    let backfills=super::backfills::inventory_sql(conn)?;
    let query=format!("SELECT path,kind,table_name,column_name FROM (
        SELECT table_name AS path,'table' AS kind,table_name,NULL AS column_name FROM _dormant_tables
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||column_name,'column',table_name,column_name FROM _dormant_columns c WHERE table_name NOT IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog t ON t.num=v.t WHERE t.name=c.table_name AND v.column_name=c.column_name)
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||index_name,'index',table_name,index_name FROM _dormant_indexes {narrowed} {backfills})
        WHERE (?1 IS NULL OR kind||':'||path>?1) ORDER BY kind||':'||path LIMIT ?2");
    let mut statement=conn.prepare(&query)?;
    let items: Vec<(String, String, String, Option<String>)> = statement
        .query_map(
            params![after, i64::try_from(limit).unwrap_or(i64::MAX)],
            |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?, r.get(2)?, r.get(3)?)),
        )?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (path, kind, name, column) in items {
        let t: Option<i64> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1", [&name], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        let Some(t) = t else { continue };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — dormant row images cover their retained cells; standalone cells have active owners.
        let (mut count,mut bytes):(u64,u64)=match kind.as_str(){
            "backfill"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(inputs)+octet_length(before_image)+octet_length(after_image)),0) FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND definition=?2",params![t,column],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "table"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "rows"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "column"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id)",params![t,column],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            _=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),0 FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1)",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
        };
        if matches!(kind.as_str(), "table" | "rows") {
            let images = if kind == "table" { "_dormant_rows" } else { "_dormant_narrow_rows" };
            bytes += conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(v.id)+octet_length(v.data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {images} d WHERE d.t=v.t AND d.id=v.id)"), [t], |r| r.get::<_, i64>(0))? as u64;
        }
        let query = match kind.as_str() {
            "backfill" => "SELECT id FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND definition=?2 ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "rows" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "table" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "column" => {
                "SELECT id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
            _ => {
                "SELECT id FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1) WHERE ?2 IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
        };
        let mut ids = conn
            .prepare(query)?
            .query_map(params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?
            .into_iter()
            .map(|id| super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id))
            .collect();
        if kind == "index" {
            (count, ids) = index_entries(conn, t, &name, column.as_deref().unwrap())?;
        }
        let asset_bytes = if kind == "index" || kind == "backfill" {
            0
        } else {
            let selector=match kind.as_str(){
                "rows"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=?3 AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",
                "column"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN r ON r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id WHERE v.t=?3 AND v.id=_assets.home_id AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column)",
                _=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows d WHERE d.t=?3 AND d.id=_assets.home_id)",
            };
            conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bytes),0) FROM _assets WHERE home_table=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR home_column=?2) AND released_at IS NULL AND {selector}"),params![name,if kind=="table" || kind=="rows" {None}else{column.as_deref()},t],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?
        };
        let definition_only = if kind == "column" {
            !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2)", params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0))?
        } else { count == 0 };
        out.push(DormantItem {
            key: format!("{kind}:{path}"),
            path,
            kind,
            action: "dormant".into(),
            rows: count,
            bytes,
            asset_bytes,
            definition_only,
            sample_ids: ids,
            before_rows:None,after_rows:None,restored_rows:None,before_sample_ids:None,
        });
    }
    Ok(out)
}

/// Retained definitions represent logical entries, rebuilt on restoration.
/// A words index has zero or many entries per image; a row count is not its size.
fn index_entries(conn: &Connection, t: i64, table: &str, index: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
    let definition: String = conn.query_row("SELECT definition FROM _dormant_indexes WHERE table_name=?1 AND index_name=?2", params![table, index], |r| r.get(0))?;
    let index: crate::schema::Index = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
    let schema: Option<String> = conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')", [], |r| r.get(0)).optional()?;
    let schema: Option<Schema> = schema.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))).transpose()?;
    let query = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id"
    } else { "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id" };
    let mut count = 0;
    let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let mut statement = conn.prepare(query)?;
    for image in statement.query_map([t], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
        let (id, data) = image?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if let Some(table) = schema.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(table)) { crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table); }
        for cell in conn.prepare("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t, id], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
            let (column, value) = cell?;
            if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
                fields.entry(column).or_insert(serde_json::from_str(&value).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?);
            }
        }
        let entries = crate::rows::index_keys(&index, &row, &id).len() as u64;
        count += entries;
        if entries > 0 && samples.len() < 5 { samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id)); }
    }
    Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — only rows surviving every activation phase count as restored.
pub(crate) fn finalize_restorations(tx:&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? { return super::live_activation::finalize(tx); }
    let entries=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%'")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,String>(0)?,r.get::<_,String>(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    let mut narrowed=0u64;
    for (key,ids) in entries {
        let (prefix,path)=key.split_once(':').expect("activation restoration key");
        let table=if prefix=="activation_column_restored_ids" {path.split_once('.').expect("column path").0} else {path};
        let count:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?2))",params![tx.num(table)?,ids],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;
        tx.set_meta(&format!("{}:{path}",prefix.trim_end_matches("_ids")),&count.to_string())?;
        if prefix=="activation_narrowed_restored_ids" {narrowed+=count;}
    }
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_restored",&narrowed.to_string())?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn event(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    operation: &str,
    release: Option<u64>,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    summary: serde_json::Value,
    items: &[DormantItem],
) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    let revision = tx
        .meta("release_revision")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .checked_add(1)
        .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("release revision exhausted".into()))?;
    tx.set_meta("release_revision", &revision.to_string())?;
    let generation = tx
        .meta("generation")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1);
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _release_events(at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,?7)",params![crate::now_ms(),operation,release.map(sql_number).transpose()?,previous.map(sql_number).transpose()?,sql_number(generation)?,sql_number(revision)?,summary.to_string()])?;
    let id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
    for item in items {
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO _release_event_items VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
            params![id, item.key, canonical_json(item)],
        )?;
    }
    Ok(revision)
}

impl Store {
    pub fn dormant_inventory(
        &mut self,
        after: Option<&str>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), after, limit)?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(items)
    }
    /// One snapshot: current identity, bounded history and storage inventories.
    pub fn release_status(
        &mut self,
        after: u64,
        items_after: Option<&str>,
        event_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        let mut inventory_page = Vec::<serde_json::Value>::new();
        if present(tx.conn())? {
            let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id,at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary FROM _release_events WHERE id>?1 ORDER BY id LIMIT 16")?;
            let mut rows = s.query([sql_number(after)?])?;
            while let Some(r) = rows.next()? {
                events.push(serde_json::json!({"id":r.get::<_,i64>(0)?,"at":r.get::<_,i64>(1)?,"operation":r.get::<_,String>(2)?,"release":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(3)?,"previous":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(4)?,"generation":r.get::<_,i64>(5)?,"revision":r.get::<_,i64>(6)?,"summary":serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&r.get::<_,String>(7)?).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?}));
            }
            if let Some(event) = event_id {
                let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT detail FROM _release_event_items WHERE event=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR path>?2) ORDER BY path LIMIT 16")?;
                for item in s.query_map(params![sql_number(event)?, items_after], |r| {
                    r.get::<_, String>(0)
                })? {
                    inventory_page.push(
                        serde_json::from_str(&item?)
                            .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?,
                    );
                }
            } else {
                inventory_page = inventory(tx.conn(), items_after, 16)?
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|i| serde_json::to_value(i).unwrap())
                    .collect();
            }
        }
        let workers:Vec<(String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key='worker:status' OR key LIKE 'worker:status:%' ORDER BY key LIMIT 16")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        let previous_active: Option<u64> = if present(tx.conn())? {tx.conn().query_row("SELECT previous FROM _release_events WHERE release IS NOT NULL AND previous != release AND operation != 'prune' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",[],|r|r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(0)).optional()?.flatten().map(|n|n as u64)} else {None};
        let result = serde_json::json!({"previous_active":previous_active,"release":tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()),"revision":tx.meta("release_revision")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"generation":tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(1),"native_digest":tx.meta("release_native_digest")?,"events_after":events.last().map(|e|e["id"].clone()),"events":events,"items_after":inventory_page.last().map(|i|i["key"].clone()),"items":inventory_page,"event":event_id,"workers":workers});
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub fn prune_dormant(
        &mut self,
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.transaction()?;
        let held = tx
            .meta("release_revision")?
            .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        if expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != held) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "release revision changed; run releases and retry prune".into(),
            ));
        }
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
        if !confirm {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(
                serde_json::json!({"pruned":false,"items":items,"rewrite":"snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive"}),
            );
        }
        if !present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":[],"revision":held}));
        }
        // Release only dormant homes; active application data is untouched.
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE home_table IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=v.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column AND v.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
        if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",[])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows",[])?;
        }
        let revision = event(
            &tx,
            "prune",
            tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()),
            None,
            serde_json::json!({"dropped_items":items.len(),"bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.bytes).sum::<u64>(),"asset_bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.asset_bytes).sum::<u64>()}),
            &items,
        )?;
        let event_id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
        super::backfills::prune(&tx)?;
        tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _dormant_values;DELETE FROM _dormant_rows;DELETE FROM _dormant_refs;DELETE FROM _dormant_columns;DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes;DELETE FROM _dormant_tables;")?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":items,"revision":revision,"event":event_id}))
    }
}

//! Bind historical arguments without requiring them in the current row type.
use super::*;
use crate::{ast, diag::{type_error, unknown}, types::{Ty, scalar_to_column}};
use snapback4_core::schema::backfill::Backfill;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    let mut names = BTreeSet::new(); let mut outputs = BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.backfills) {
        if !names.insert((d.table.clone(),d.name.clone())) { diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,"duplicate backfill name on this table","give each historical transform its own name")); continue; }
        let decl = ast::ProgramDecl { delivery: None, held:false, predicts:false, server:false, kind:ast::ProgramKind::Query,
            name: format!("backfill{}{}",d.table,d.name), args:d.args.clone(), prelude:vec![],
            body:vec![ast::Stmt::Return { value:d.value.clone(), site:d.site.clone() }], native:false, site:d.site.clone() };
        let Some(bound) = program::Binder::bind(catalog,schema,"schema",&decl,diagnostics) else {continue};
        let Ty::Object(fields) = &bound.result else { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,"backfill returns an object patch","{ column: historicalValue }")); continue; };
        let Some(table) = schema.table(&d.table) else {diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,"backfill table is not declared","declare the candidate table"));continue};
        for (c,ty) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(c) {
                if !ty.assignable_to(&Ty::from_column(kind)) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfill {}.{c} returns {}, expected {}",d.table,ty.name(),kind),"return a value fitting the candidate column")); }
            }
            if !outputs.insert((d.table.clone(),c.clone())) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfills overlap on {}.{c}",d.table),"combine simultaneous outputs in one backfill")); }
        }
        let fill = Backfill { table:d.table.clone(), name:d.name.clone(), inputs:d.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),scalar_to_column(&a.type_))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(), outputs:fields.keys().cloned().collect(), program:bound.program };
        if let Err(why) = fill.admit(schema) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,why,"use deterministic historical fields and a scalar patch").guide("deploy")); }
        schema.backfills.push(fill.normalized());
    }
}
//! Human preview groups facts about one affected table/operation once.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#15-wave-15-preview-and-rollback-day5 — full evidence remains available in JSON and verbose output.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::{Schema, ir::Program, store::DormantItem, successor::Step};
struct Group { warnings: Vec<String>, notes: Vec<String>, priority: u8 }
impl Default for Group { fn default() -> Self {Self {warnings:Vec::new(),notes:Vec::new(),priority:2}} }
fn table_prefix(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
    tables.iter().filter(|name|text.strip_prefix(name.as_str()).is_some_and(|tail|tail.is_empty() || tail.starts_with(['.',':',' ']))).max_by_key(|name|name.len()).cloned()
}
fn subject(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>) -> String {
    // Read only generated subject positions, never arbitrary words inside an
    // authored predicate/refusal/value. Unknown formats remain release facts.
    if let Some(table)=table_prefix(text,tables) {return table;}
    for marker in ["table ","dependent ","backfill: "] {
        if let Some(tail)=text.strip_prefix(marker) && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    let prefix=text.split(';').next().unwrap_or(text);
    if text.starts_with("queries/counts ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" read ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("release ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" safety dependencies on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("rollback users get release ") {
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" quotas on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once("'s ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    if text.starts_with("release ") {
        if let Some(token)=prefix.split_whitespace().last() && let Some((owner,effect))=token.rsplit_once('.') {
            if effect=="require" {return format!("operation {owner}");}
            if tables.contains(owner) && matches!(effect,"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete") {return owner.into();}
        }
        for name in programs.keys() {
            if prefix.ends_with(&format!(" operation {name}")) {return format!("operation {name}");}
        }
    }
    "release behavior".into()
}
fn brief(text: &str) -> String {
    // Semicolons also separate authored guards and multiple cap declarations;
    // retaining the full fact avoids dropping their contracts or caveats.
    text.to_owned()
}
pub(super) fn print(current: Option<&Schema>, target: &Schema, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>, warnings: &[String], notes: &[String], steps: &[Step], items: &[DormantItem]) {
    let tables: BTreeSet<_>=target.tables.keys().chain(current.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.tables.keys())).cloned().collect();
    let mut groups=BTreeMap::<String,Group>::new();
    for (warning, lines) in [(true,warnings),(false,notes)] {
        for line in lines {
            let group=groups.entry(subject(line,&tables,programs)).or_default();
            let priority=if line.contains("safety guard") || line.contains(".require;") {0} else if line.contains("confidentiality") || line.contains("safety rule") {1} else {2};
            group.priority=group.priority.min(priority);
            let text=if priority==0 {line.clone()} else {brief(line)};
            let dest=if warning {&mut group.warnings} else {&mut group.notes};
            if !dest.contains(&text) {dest.push(text);}
        }
    }
    for step in steps {
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path);
        let key=table_prefix(path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||path.to_owned());
        if path.strip_prefix(&key).is_some_and(|tail|tail.starts_with(".indexes.")) {continue;}
        let group=groups.entry(key).or_default();
        // Keep each user-visible source change even when another fact already
        // names this table. Only index plumbing is verbose-only.
        let detail=brief(&format!("{}: {}",path.replace(".columns.","."),step.message));
        if !group.notes.contains(&detail) {group.notes.push(detail);}
    }
    let mut storage=BTreeMap::<String,[u64;3]>::new();
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.rows>0 && item.kind!="backfill") {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        let slot=match item.kind.as_str() {"column"=>1,"index"=>2,_=>0};
        storage.entry(name).or_default()[slot]+=item.rows;
    }
    for (name,[rows,cells,indexes]) in storage {
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {rows} dormant rows, {cells} cells, {indexes} index entries"));
    }
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.kind=="backfill" && item.rows>0) {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {} private backfill preimages ({} bytes)",item.rows,item.bytes));
    }
    let mut groups=groups.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    groups.sort_by_key(|(name,g)|(g.priority,name.clone()));
    for (name,mut group) in groups {
        let warning=!group.warnings.is_empty();
        group.warnings.append(&mut group.notes);
        println!("  {} {name}: {}{}",if warning {"WARNING"} else {"note:"},group.warnings.join("; "),if warning {"; verify these cases or preserve the current behavior in a bridge release"} else {""});
    }
}

#[test]
fn authored_values_cannot_assign_a_guard_to_an_unrelated_table() {
    let tables=BTreeSet::from(["unrelated".to_owned(),"workspace.plans".to_owned()]);
    let line="release 1 loosens book.require; old: require title != 'unrelated' else STOP; new: allow; rollback users lose a current safety guard";
    assert_eq!(subject(line,&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"operation book");
    assert_eq!(subject("workspace.plans.state: 2 rows become dormant",&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"workspace.plans");
}

Restoration identity sets are held transactionally and counted against final surviving r only after all read-through/cache activation phases. There is no automatic collection.
llp/3000.000.000-retained-storage-and-hot-release.rfc.md:250:### 9.2 Exact preview and operator vocabulary
llp/3000.000.000-retained-storage-and-hot-release.rfc.md:252:`rollback <dir> --to N --check` and `check --store <path> --release N` execute candidate adoption on a private SQLite snapshot created by VACUUM INTO through a read-only source connection. This is disk-backed, uses the exact adoption builder/validation, and never changes the live source, pointer, events or generation. SQLite supports VACUUM INTO on a read-only source; it acquires a coherent WAL read snapshot without the source writer lock. BUSY/disk failures refuse with retry advice, never fall back to copying sidecars. The temporary directory is owner-only and removed on success, refusal and ordinary unwinding. Process kill/crash may leave a private preview directory; operators can remove snapback4-release-preview-* leftovers when no preview uses them. Complete here means a numeric directory with nonempty source that compiles; empty permanently reserved crash directories are skipped. Native preparation still validates a selected release before activation. Packaging and comparing the content digest precedes allocating a numeric release. llp/3000.000.000-retained-storage-and-hot-release.rfc.md:809:The proposed child [LLP 3000.000.000.002](./3000.000.000.002-declarative-backfills.rfc.md)
            )));
        }
        if let Some(through) = &table.through {
            {
                let mut bad = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
                super::activation_walk::walk_metered(tx,t,crate::rows::adoption::Work::DECODE,None,|id,row,meter| {
                    let restored: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
                        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
                        params![t, id],
                        |r| r.get(0),
                    )?;
                    if !restored {
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    // The captured deadline belongs to one parent lifetime,
                    // even if an older schema allowed retargeting the active ref.
                    let parent_t = tx.num(&through.parent)?;
                    let current = row
                        .field(&through.reference)
                        .ok()
                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok());
                    let mut evidence=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT parent_t,parent_id,(SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=parent_t) FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3")?;
                    for edge in evidence.query_map(
                        params![t, id, crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE],
                        |r| {
                            Ok((
                                r.get::<_, i64>(0)?,
                                r.get::<_, String>(1)?,
                                r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
                            ))
                        },
                    )? {
                        let (old_table, old_id, old_name) = edge?;
                        if old_table != parent_t || current != Some(old_id.as_str()) {
                            bad.add(
                                id,
                                &format!(
                                    "{}.{}: recorded {} [{}], current {} [{}]",
                                    name,
                                    through.reference,
                                    old_name,
                                    old_id,
                                    through.parent,
                                    current.unwrap_or("<missing>")
                                ),
                            );
                            return Ok(());
                        }
                    }
                    if let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference) {
                        if through.live.is_some() || crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? {
                            let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
                            meter.heap((bytes.unwrap_or(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(4096))?;
                        }
                        if let Some(parent) = tx.get(&through.parent, parent_id)? {
                            let parent =
                                crate::schema::row_image(&parent, &next.tables[&through.parent]);
                            // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#71-dormant-restoration-identities-are-a-relation — live policy restamps current inputs after preserving parent lifetime.
                            if through.live.is_none() && !crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? && through.fields.iter().any(|(capture, field)| {
                                row.field(capture)
                                    .ok()
                                    .is_some_and(|value| Some(value) != parent.field(field).ok())
                            }) {
                                bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} parent {parent_id}: captured audience/identity changed",name,through.reference));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                if bad.count > 0 {
                    return Err(refuse(format!(
                        "table {name}: {}; restore cannot rebind historical audience/deadline captures; restore the original parent reference and audience under the current release, then `snapback4 rollback <dir> --to N`, or explicitly `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`",
                        bad.describe()
                    )));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    super::backfills::delete(tx,t,id)?;
    if !enabled(tx)? {
        return Ok(());
    }
    tx.conn().execute(
        "UPDATE _dormant_refs SET invalid=1 WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2 AND invalid=0",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    let narrowed=super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())?;
    let home=if narrowed {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2)"} else {""};
    tx.conn().execute(&format!("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?3) WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_id=?2 AND (home_column IN (SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2){home})"),params![name,id,crate::now_ms(),t])?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    if narrowed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;}
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn holds_home(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, column: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if !enabled(tx)? {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    let narrowed=if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON n.t=c.num WHERE c.name=?1)"}else{""};
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_tables WHERE table_name=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2){narrowed}"),params![table,column],|r|r.get(0))?)
}

/// Fetch only destination cells on live transitions, after paying their full
/// serialized/decoded coexistence in the owning activation page/row account.
pub(super) fn restored_values(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,meter:&mut crate::meter::Meter)->Result<Vec<(String,String)>,RuntimeError> {
    if !super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
        return Ok(tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?);
    }
    let mut values=Vec::new();
    for column in table.columns.keys() {
        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        if let Some(bytes)=bytes {
            meter.heap((bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(1024+column.len() as u64*8))?;
            let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
            values.push((column.clone(),data));
        }
    }
    Ok(values)
}

/// Narrowed forward correspondence has already run when this is called.
/// Record cell-derived live provenance before consuming any source evidence.
pub(super) fn mark_live_column(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? && super::authority_origin::mode(tx,t,id)? != Some(1) {
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',?1,'',?2 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=1)",params![t,id,column])?;
    }Ok(())
}

//! The generation barrier checks predecessor audiences before destructive projection.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#7-activation — pinned time, child scan, capture before validation, atomic publication.
use crate::{
    error::RuntimeError,
    rows::{Bounds, Dir, Rows},
    schema::{RuleExpr, Schema, Table, through::PARENT_DEADLINE},
    value::Value,
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

const EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR: &str = "$expiry-table";

/// Rotate a sweep's table order after the last table that consumed a bounded
/// visit. Per-table row cursors alone can starve every later table when an
/// earlier table contains a permanently restricted due row.
pub(crate) fn resume_expiry_tables(
    mut tables: Vec<String>,
    cursors: &BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let after = cursors
        .get(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR)
        .and_then(|cursor| cursor.first())
        .and_then(|value| value.as_str().ok());
    if let Some(index) = after.and_then(|after| tables.iter().position(|table| table == after)) {
        let next = (index + 1) % tables.len().max(1);
        tables.rotate_left(next);
    }
    tables
}

pub(crate) fn mark_expiry_table(cursors: &mut BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>, table: &str) {
    cursors.insert(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR.into(), vec![Value::String(table.into())]);
}

/// Only inherited batches need staging. Preserve the streaming path for
/// existing schemas, including large fixture imports.
pub(crate) fn ancestor_order<'a, I>(
    schema: &Schema,
    rows: I,
) -> Box<dyn Iterator<Item = (String, Value)> + 'a>
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
    I::IntoIter: 'a,
{
    if schema.tables.values().all(|t| t.through.is_none()) {
        return Box::new(rows.into_iter());
    }
    let order = schema.through_order();
    let mut rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
    rows.sort_by_key(|(name, _)| order.iter().position(|n| n == name).unwrap_or(usize::MAX));
    Box::new(rows.into_iter())
}

fn refusal(name: &str, count: usize, reason: &str, samples: &[String]) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_READ_THROUGH_ADOPT",
        message: format!(
            "{name}: {count} child rows cannot adopt read through: {reason}; sample ids/parent values [{}]; rewrite: reconcile the named audience fields and live parents in a bridge release, then `snapback4 deploy <dir> --restore-dormant` (guide rules)",
            samples.join(", ")
        ),
    }
}

fn sample(samples: &mut Vec<String>, row: &Value, reference: &str, parent: &str) {
    fn bounded(text: &str) -> &str {
        let mut end = text.len().min(256);
        while !text.is_char_boundary(end) {
            end -= 1;
        }
        &text[..end]
    }
    if samples.len() < 5 {
        let id = row
            .field("id")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        let value = row
            .field(reference)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        samples.push(format!(
            "{}: {reference} -> {parent}[{}]",
            bounded(id),
            bounded(value)
        ));
    }
}

fn live_work(tx:&dyn Rows,name:&str,old:Option<&Table>,next:&Schema)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || tx.has_restored_live_authority(name)?)
}

// Both definitions may coexist in a raw source image until projection.
fn parent_bytes(a:Option<&Table>,b:Option<&Table>)->u64 {
    a.into_iter().chain(b).map(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).fold(0_u64,u64::saturating_add).saturating_mul(4)
}

fn rows(
    tx: &dyn Rows, name: &str, table: &Table, parent_scratch: u64, bounded: bool, destination: Option<&Table>,
    mut visit: impl FnMut(Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !bounded {
        let mut bounds=Bounds::default();
        loop {
            let page=tx.scan(name,"byId",&[],&bounds,Dir::Asc,256)?;
            if page.is_empty() {return Ok(());}
            for (row,key) in page {bounds.after=crate::key::decode(&key);visit(row)?;}
        }
    }
    let decode=crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table);
    let keys=crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,|t|crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,t)));
    let entries=crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity(table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity)).saturating_mul(2);
    let scratch=decode.saturating_mul(4).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).saturating_mul(4)).saturating_add(keys.saturating_mul(2))
        .saturating_add(parent_scratch).saturating_add(8192);
    tx.walk_adoption(name,decode,scratch,[2,table.columns.len() as u64+16,entries],&mut visit)
}

pub(crate) fn correspondence(
    child: &RuleExpr,
    parent: &RuleExpr,
) -> Option<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    if child.is_uniformly_readable() && parent.is_uniformly_readable() {
        return Some(Default::default());
    }
    fn walk(
        a: &serde_json::Value,
        b: &serde_json::Value,
        map: &mut std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>,
    ) -> bool {
        use serde_json::Value::*;
        match (a, b) {
            (Object(a), Object(b)) if a.len() == b.len() => {
                if let (Some(String(a)), Some(String(b))) = (a.get("Field"), b.get("Field")) {
                    if map.get(a).is_some_and(|old| old != b)
                        || map.iter().any(|(old, v)| v == b && old != a)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                    map.insert(a.clone(), b.clone());
                    true
                } else {
                    a.iter()
                        .all(|(k, a)| b.get(k).is_some_and(|b| walk(a, b, map)))
                }
            }
            (Array(a), Array(b)) if a.len() == b.len() => {
                a.iter().zip(b).all(|(a, b)| walk(a, b, map))
            }
            _ => a == b,
        }
    }
    let mut map = Default::default();
    walk(
        &serde_json::to_value(child).ok()?,
        &serde_json::to_value(parent).ok()?,
        &mut map,
    )
    .then_some(map)
}

/// The retirement phase shared by activation and its read-only occupancy
/// preview. Selection and the predecessor deadline predicate live here once;
/// checking a candidate must not count a row this phase would remove.
pub(crate) struct Retirement<'a> {
    affected: Vec<(String, &'a Table)>,
    now: i64,
}

impl<'a> Retirement<'a> {
    pub(crate) fn new(next: &Schema, previous: &'a Schema, now: i64) -> Self {
        let affected = next
            .through_order()
            .into_iter()
            .filter_map(|name| {
                let next = &next.tables[&name];
                let old = previous.table(&name)?;
                Self::changes_lifetime(next,old).then_some((name, old))
            })
            .collect();
        Self { affected, now }
    }

    fn changes_lifetime(next:&Table,old:&Table) -> bool {
        next.through != old.through || next.through.is_some() && (next.expire != old.expire || next.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field)))
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires_image(next:&Table,old:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64) -> bool {
        (next.through!=old.through || next.expire!=old.expire || old.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field))) && old.expired(row,now)
    }

    pub(crate) fn affects(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.affected.iter().any(|(table, _)| table == name)
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires(&self, name: &str, row: &Value) -> bool {
        self.affected
            .iter()
            .find(|(table, _)| table == name)
            .is_some_and(|(_, old)| old.expired(row, self.now))
    }
}

/// Capture barrier for an authoritative adapter's uncommitted generation.
pub fn activate(
    tx: &dyn Rows,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, u64>, RuntimeError> {
    activate_retained(tx, next, previous, now, &Default::default())
}

pub(crate) fn activate_retained(
    tx: &dyn Rows,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
    retained: &std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, u64>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut shortened = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    let Some(previous) = previous else {
        return Ok(shortened);
    };
    // Previous indexes and row images still exist. Every affected expired row
    // retires under its old deadline, before a new default or capture can revive it.
    let retirement = Retirement::new(next, previous, now);

/// Missing optional columns read as null; missing required columns with a
/// literal default read that value. Contextual defaults belong to inserts.
pub fn materialize_optional_fields(row: &mut Value, table: &Table) {
    let fields = match row {
        Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) => fields,
        _ => return,
    };
    for (name, kind) in &table.columns {
        if !fields.contains_key(name) {
            if table.maintained.contains_key(name) {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), Value::Int(0));
            } else if kind.is_optional() {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), Value::Null);
            } else if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value)) = table.defaults.get(name) {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), value.clone());
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Borrow complete images; materialize old images only when needed.
pub fn row_image<'a>(row: &'a Value, table: &Table) -> std::borrow::Cow<'a, Value> {
    if table.columns.keys().all(|column| row.field(column).is_ok()) { return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(row); }
    let mut image = row.clone();
    materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, table);
    std::borrow::Cow::Owned(image)
}

    pub fn cap(&self, by: &[String]) -> Option<u32> {
        self.constraints.iter().find_map(|constraint| match constraint {
            Constraint::Cap { by: group, max } if group == by && !self.cap_exempts_null(group) => Some(*max),
            _ => None,
        })
    }

    /// A cap grouping by a `set null` reference does not count its null rows
    /// (LLP 3000.039 §2.2), so it proves no population bound.
    pub fn cap_exempts_null(&self, by: &[String]) -> bool {
        by.iter().any(|column| self.on_delete.get(column) == Some(&references::OnDelete::SetNull))
    }

    pub fn immutable_fields(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &str> {
        self.constraints.iter().flat_map(|constraint| match constraint {
            Constraint::Immutable { fields } => fields.iter().map(String::as_str).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            _ => Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    /// Whether `row` is past its `expire` column at `now`: absent to every
    /// reader, pending the sweep. A null time never expires.
    pub fn expired(&self, row: &Value, now: i64) -> bool {
        self.deadline(row).is_some_and(|at| at <= now)
    }

    /// The index the expiry sweep walks: the first leading with the
    /// `expire` column, as the declaration requires.
    pub fn expiry_index(&self) -> Option<(&str, &Index)> {
        let column = self.expiry_column()?;
        self.indexes.iter().find(|(_, index)| {
                index.is_plain() && index.columns().first().map(String::as_str) == Some(column)
            }).map(|(name, index)| (name.as_str(), index))
//! Lifetime-bound rule inputs, distinct from historical recorded attribution.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — captures have their parent's type and activation lifecycle.
use super::*;

pub const PARENT_DEADLINE: &str = "$parentDeadline";
pub const DEADLINE: &str = "$deadline";
pub const CHILDREN: &str = "$children";
pub const LIVE_CHILDREN: &str = "$liveChildren";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Through {
    /// Bounded live maintenance; absence preserves captured wire bytes.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub live: Option<u32>,
    pub reference: String,
    pub parent: String,
    /// Child engine field -> immediate parent field (including its captures).
    pub fields: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub identity: String,
    pub expiring: bool,
}

impl Table {
    pub fn engine_field(&self, field: &str) -> bool {
        (self.ephemeral.is_some() && field == DEADLINE) || self.recorded.contains_key(field)
            || self.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| {
                t.fields.contains_key(field) || matches!(field, DEADLINE | PARENT_DEADLINE)
            })
    }
    /// One classification for every expiry gate, including inherited deadlines.
    pub fn is_expiring(&self) -> bool {
        self.ephemeral.is_some() || self.expire.is_some() || self.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring)
    }
    pub fn expiry_column(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        if self.ephemeral.is_some() || self.through.is_some() && self.is_expiring() {
            Some(DEADLINE)
        } else {
            self.expire.as_deref()
        }
    }
    pub fn deadline(&self, row: &Value) -> Option<i64> {
        match self
            .expiry_column()
            .and_then(|field| row_image(row, self).field(field).ok().cloned())
        {
            Some(Value::Int(at)) => Some(at),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    /// Never trust a restored effective instant; recompute from immutable capture.
    pub fn recompute_deadline(&self, row: &mut Value) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.through.is_none() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let authored = self
            .expire
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|c| match row_image(row, self).field(c) {
                Ok(Value::Int(at)) => Some(*at),
                _ => None,
            });
        let parent = match row.field(PARENT_DEADLINE)? {
            Value::Int(at) => Some(*at),
            Value::Null => None,
            _ => {
                return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Input(
                    "captured parent deadline must be a time or null".into(),
                ));
            }
        };
        let deadline = authored
            .into_iter()
            .chain(parent)
            .min()
            .map(Value::Int)
            .unwrap_or(Value::Null);
        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = row {
            fields.insert(DEADLINE.into(), deadline);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
